# Open-Source 3D LUT Mega-Vault (iPhone 17 Blackmagic Camera & Cinema Edition)

**Total Unique, Binary-Verified & Auto-Labeled `.cube` LUTs:** `2235`

Every LUT in this repository is SHA-256 deduplicated, verified by 3D grid dimension (`17pt`/`33pt` mobile vs. `65pt` desktop), and labeled by **Color Space** and **Visual Style**.

## Visual LUT Contact Sheets (3D Trilinear Color Swatch Previews)

### 1. iPhone 17 Pro / Pro Max — Apple Log 2 (`luts/1_Apple_Log_2_iPhone17Pro`)
![Apple Log 2 Previews](previews/preview_apple_log_2.png)

### 2. iPhone Pro — Original Apple Log (`luts/2_Apple_Log_Original`)
![Apple Log Previews](previews/preview_apple_log_v1.png)

### 3. Base iPhone 17 / 17 Air — Rec. 709 & Film Emulation (`luts/3_Rec709_Creative_Looks`)
![Rec709 Previews](previews/preview_rec709_creative.png)

### 4. Universal Camera Logs & Kodak 2383 / Fuji 3513 Print (`luts/4_Camera_Log_and_Film_Print`)
![Camera Log Previews](previews/preview_camera_log_print.png)

## Folder Architecture
- `luts/1_Apple_Log_2_iPhone17Pro/` — Strictly 17pt/33pt Mobile-Ready LUTs for iPhone 17 Pro / Pro Max (Apple Log 2 Wide Gamut)
- `luts/2_Apple_Log_Original/` — Strictly 17pt/33pt Mobile-Ready LUTs for iPhone Pro (Apple Log)
- `luts/3_Rec709_Creative_Looks/` — Strictly <=36pt Mobile-Ready Creative & Film Looks for Base iPhone 17 / Rec. 709
- `luts/4_Camera_Log_and_Film_Print/` — Strictly <=36pt Kodak 2383, Fuji 3513, ARRI LogC, Sony S-Log2/3, V-Log, F-Log2 & DJI D-Log LUTs
- `luts/5_Desktop_Mastering_65pt/` — High-precision 64pt/65pt Desktop Mastering LUTs (DaVinci Resolve / Premiere / FCP)
